EVIDENCE / PER GAME

What actually ran.

Every environment has a separate execution receipt. Source import, numerical parity, original-horizon execution, render filtering, browser control and human performance remain separate claims.

256/256 native full-episode checks and 256/256 browser-control checks passed. Floating parity is exact for 179 identities. 77 have measured nonzero eager/JIT differences and remain numerically undetermined; no arbitrary acceptance tolerance was introduced. Human scores remain null.
